cm-outbound-bench -- manual publication manifest
==============================================================================

repository root : D:/flowchart-baseline-dist/cm-outbound-bench
target branch   : research/milestone-1.2-clean
tracked files   : 1327
untracked / new : 3744
total           : 5071 files

Lists every file in the working tree that is NOT ignored, i.e. every file
the researcher may consider for manual staging. Nothing has been staged,
committed or pushed.

Class 1 = approved processed research data
Class 2 = restricted evaluator data (hidden labels, scoring, traces,
          annotation packets); keep separated from Agent-visible Tasks

OWNER CONFIRMATION REQUIRED: every file under data/decision/ and
outputs/decision/ is included SUBJECT TO the owner confirming that private
GitHub hosting is within the permitted research-use boundary.
